public function integer get_array_index_value ();int li_rec  //, wcheck_flag 
dec WCOLECT_NO
WCOLECT_NO 䅄⩔ꠀJǶ= 0
warray_index = 0
For li_rec = 1 to  dw_1.rowcount()
	wcheck_flag= dw_1.getitemdecimal(li_rec,"seq")
	WCOLECT_NO= dw_1.getitemdecimal(li_rec,"cust_no")
	if wcheck_flag > 0 then 
		warray_index = warray_index +1
		WFSTORE_NO_COLECT[warray_ind䅄⩔ꨀJǶex ] = WCOLECT_NO
	end if	 
next  
if warray_index= 0  then 
	f_message (WFAPPL_TYPE , WFAPPL_SUB ,"011",w_msg_1)
	dw_1.setfocus()
	return -1
end if	 

end function
